独立评测发现

来源：独立评审 · 口径 v2 · 源 Run 38481201050 / attempt 1

最新上游状态：未复核。原评审“已解决”不作为上游修复证明；应用绕行、业务 QA 通过均不关闭框架问题。

等级与类型怎么定
  • 严重阻塞交付，或造成数据丢失、安全问题，且没有合理绕行
  • 重要按指引或公开 API 正常使用会静默得到错误结果，或只能用非公开手段绕行
  • 轻微能完成，但要多花排查成本或在应用侧绕行
  • 信息措辞、示例完整度等不影响结果的改进
  • 实现缺陷实现违背文档或公开约定
  • 能力缺口需求属于框架职责，但缺少能力或公开接入
  • 指引错误指引、示例或注释写错，或彼此矛盾，照做会出错
  • 指引缺失指引缺少必要说明或示例，需要读源码或试探
  • 易用性改进现有能力可用，但有改进空间

NocoBase3 库、插件、模板与指引 1

轻微易用性改进上游未复核反馈复核：证据支持原评审置信度：confirmed
F3 · Scheduler 管理 API 无“立即执行/触发”入口，验证与运维只能等待 cron

NocoBase3 内核 · @nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-scheduler/references/operations.md · 原评审：未解决

⌄

反馈证据核对

冻结源码与文档一致证实：Scheduler 插件管理 API 只有 list/get/occurrences/enable/disable，公开服务无触发方法；应用只能靠 cron '* * * * *' 等待真实 firing，浏览器验收也记录“无立即执行入口，依赖 Cron”。这是框架能力边界上的可用性改进，不涉及违约或应用缺陷。

  • 职责与公开约定：冻结公开约定：scheduler Skill operations.md 列出管理入口仅 GET /schedules、GET /schedules/:scheduleId、GET /schedules/:scheduleId/occurrences、POST .../enable、POST .../disable，并明确写“The API supports viewing and enabling/disabling tasks, with no route for creating or editing Cron definitions”；验证指引要求“In development, use a short Cron interval to observe an execution”。该约定没有承诺 trigger/立即执行端点，故本候选属可用性建议而非违约。框架职责边界：插件提供管理 API 与 target/occurrence 协议，应用自建页面。 E18 E6
  • 实际行为证据：实现实际行为与结论相符：routes/index.js 只注册上述五条路由（enable/disable 调 scheduler.setEnabled），未注册任何 trigger/run 路由；DefaultSchedulerService 公开方法为 defineSchedule/registerTarget/list/get/listOccurrences/sync/setEnabled/activate/recordEvent/reconcileOccurrences，无 trigger 方法；应用用 cron '* * * * *' 取得真实 firing。浏览器验收 B02 直接记录“等待同一计划由真实调度再次执行（无立即执行入口，依赖 Cron * * * * *）”。因此“只能启用并等待 cron”在管理 API 范围内成立。 E6 E51 E52 E28
  • 应用接入与业务边界：应用未错误装配或误用 API：app/server/routes/index.ts 只挂载 todos 业务路由，未尝试解析 scheduler 私有 store/executor 或自行触发；app/server/providers/todos.ts 用 registerTarget + defineSchedule(cron '* * * * *') 正常装配，与插件公开面一致。缺入口归因于框架公开 API，而非应用遗漏业务逻辑。 E53 E52 E18
  • 环境因素：无环境因素更能解释该现象：同一环境下浏览器验收已观察到真实 cron firing（Succeeded result {"updated":1} 与 {"updated":0}），说明外部服务、jobs 适配器与运行环境都能正常触发；缺少的只是按需触发入口，而该入口在插件源码公开面本就不存在，与环境无关。 E28 E6
  • 工厂因素：工厂侧：scheduler 插件及其 Skill 由本轮冻结材料提供，operations.md 主动声明“no route for creating or editing Cron definitions”并建议短周期 cron 验证，未承诺 trigger；需求也把“立即执行”写成条件项（如本轮提供则须走同一 Scheduler 目标及执行记录）。因此该缺口是框架公开能力边界，不是工厂提示、注入配置或 QA/构建脚本覆盖造成的误配。 E18
  • 已有能力与推荐用法：已核既有公开入口、正常组合与文档：schedulerServiceToken 仅暴露 registerTarget/defineSchedule 作为贡献面，管理读改经 HTTP 五路由；operations.md 明确 store、target registry、schedule executor 私有且不可由容器解析，应用无法在不绕过授权与 occurrence 的情况下触发。底层 @nocobase/jobs 的 ScheduleJobOption.immediately 仅“Fires once on creation; Ignored for updates”，且 scheduler 的 ScheduleDefinition 未暴露该字段、createScheduleDispatchJob 也未传入；CLI scheduler sync 只同步。故无既有能力满足“按需触发一次真实 firing 并写入 occurrence”。 E18 E51 E54 E55

反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

公共接口只有 list/get/occurrences/enable/disable，没有触发一次 firing 的路由或服务方法；本轮以 cron * * * * * 等待真实触发，浏览器验收也记录了“无立即执行入口，依赖 Cron”。

实际影响
运维与验收在需要即时验证或补偿执行时只能等待下一个周期，长周期计划尤其不便。
建议改法
可选地在管理 API 增加受 page:scheduler.schedules/access 保护的“trigger once”端点，走同一 target 与 occurrence 记录（不作为业务写库后门）。
诊断 · 触发条件、应有行为、绕行代价与验收标准
触发条件
管理员或验收者希望立即验证一次调度
应有能力 / 行为
有一个受授权保护的入口触发一次真实 firing 并写入 occurrence
实际观察
只能启用计划并等待 cron 到点
应用绕行与代价
使用短周期 cron（如 * * * * *）并等待一分钟
改进后的验收标准
触发后出现新的 occurrence 且 target 通过统一协议执行
评审时证据 · 冻结快照 3 条
packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler/dist/server/routes/index.js:85–144E6

occurrences 游标分页、enable/disable 路由：POST 调 scheduler.setEnabled(id, true/false) 并返回更新后的 Schedule。

    schedules.get('/schedules/:scheduleId/occurrences', describeRoute({
        tags,
        summary: 'List the occurrences of a Schedule',
        operationId: 'schedulerListOccurrences',
        description: `The firings of a Schedule, newest first. Pages by \`pageToken\`: pass \`meta.nextPageToken\` back unchanged; it is absent on the last page. ${accessDescription}`,
        responses: {
            200: listResponse(ScheduleOccurrenceSchema),
            ...apiErrorResponses,
            404: scheduleNotFoundResponse,
        },
    }), scheduleParams, apiValidator('query', OccurrenceListQuery), async (context) => {
        const { scheduleId } = context.req.valid('param');
        const { pageSize, pageToken=[REDACTED] } = context.req.valid('query');
        if (!(await scheduler.get(scheduleId)))
            throw scheduleNotFound(scheduleId);
        // One row past the page tells whether another page follows without counting the whole history.
        const rows = await scheduler.listOccurrences(scheduleId, {
            offset,
            limit: pageSize + 1,
        });
        const hasMore = rows.length > pageSize;
        return context.json({
            data: rows.slice(0, pageSize),
            meta: hasMore
                ? { nextPageToken=[REDACTED] + pageSize) }
                : {},
        });
    });
    schedules.post('/schedules/:scheduleId/enable', describeRoute({
        tags,
        summary: 'Enable a Schedule',
        operationId: 'schedulerEnableSchedule',
        description: `Switches the Schedule on and plans its next firing when its lifecycle is active. ${accessDescription}`,
        responses: {
            200: dataResponse(ScheduleSchema, 'The Schedule after the change.'),
            ...apiErrorResponses,
            404: scheduleNotFoundResponse,
        },
    }), scheduleParams, async (context) => {
        const { scheduleId } = context.req.valid('param');
        return context.json({
            data: await scheduler.setEnabled(scheduleId, true),
        });
    });
    schedules.post('/schedules/:scheduleId/disable', describeRoute({
        tags,
        summary: 'Disable a Schedule',
        operationId: 'schedulerDisableSchedule',
        description: `Switches the Schedule off and removes its planned firing; the definition stays. ${accessDescription}`,
        responses: {
            200: dataResponse(ScheduleSchema, 'The Schedule after the change.'),
            ...apiErrorResponses,
            404: scheduleNotFoundResponse,
        },
    }), scheduleParams, async (context) => {
        const { scheduleId } = context.req.valid('param');
        return context.json({
            data: await scheduler.setEnabled(scheduleId, false),
        });
    });
app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-scheduler/references/operations.md:9–41E18

运维指南：插件不提供页面，应用基于 GET/POST 管理路由自建页面；所有路由要求 page:scheduler.schedules/access，且先鉴权后查找；无创建/编辑 Cron 的路由。

## Management Entry Points

The plugin contributes no pages: the application builds its schedule list and detail pages on the routes below and declares them among its own routes, guarded by the same permission. Definitions are maintained in source. The API supports viewing and enabling/disabling tasks, with no route for creating or editing Cron definitions.

All routes below require authentication and authorization for `{ resource: { type: 'page', id: 'scheduler.schedules' }, action: 'access' }`, checked before the schedule is looked up. Enabling/disabling currently uses the same permission, not a separate update action.

| Method and path                                        | Response / effect                                                                                                                                                  |
| ------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `GET /api/scheduler/schedules`                         | `{ data: ScheduleListItem[], meta: { page, pageSize, total } }`; `page` (default 1) and `pageSize` (default 20, at most 100)                                       |
| `GET /api/scheduler/schedules/:scheduleId`             | `{ data: ScheduleListItem }`                                                                                                                                       |
| `GET /api/scheduler/schedules/:scheduleId/occurrences` | `{ data: ScheduleOccurrenceView[], meta: { nextPageToken? } }`, newest first; `pageSize` (default 20, at most 100) and `pageToken` from the previous page's `meta` |
| `POST /api/scheduler/schedules/:scheduleId/enable`     | `{ data: ScheduleListItem }`; enables the task, no request body                                                                                                    |
| `POST /api/scheduler/schedules/:scheduleId/disable`    | `{ data: ScheduleListItem }`; pauses the task, no request body                                                                                                     |

Failures use the standard `/api` error body with domain `scheduler`: a caller without page access gets `403` with reason `SCHEDULE_ACCESS_REQUIRED`, an id this application has no schedule for gets `404` with reason `SCHEDULE_NOT_FOUND`, and an invalid `page`, `pageSize` or `pageToken` gets `400` with reason `INVALID_INPUT`. Treat `pageToken` as opaque; the last page has no `nextPageToken`.

The running application documents these routes under the `Scheduler` tag at `/api/swagger/docs` (JSON at `/api/swagger`, signed in), with the full `ScheduleListItem` and occurrence schemas; their operationIds are `schedulerListSchedules`, `schedulerGetSchedule`, `schedulerListOccurrences`, `schedulerEnableSchedule` and `schedulerDisableSchedule`.

Use the id returned by list for `:scheduleId`, not the definition key or a target config value. Enabling/disabling affects future scheduling; it does not cancel work already started.

`schedulerServiceToken` from `@nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler/server/tokens` is the plugin's one public service, and it is a contribution surface rather than an administration one:

```ts
registerTarget<TConfig extends JsonObject>(
  target: ScheduleTargetType<TConfig>,
): ScheduleTargetHandle;

defineSchedule(definition: ScheduleDefinition): void;
```

The returned handle carries `reportCompletion(occurrenceId, reference, completion)`, and completes only occurrences its own target started. `defineSchedule` has no return value; it validates and stores the definition in the in-memory manifest consumed by the next sync.

Reading and changing schedules — list, occurrences, enable and disable — is reachable through the HTTP API above, and synchronization through `pnpm nocobase scheduler sync`. Neither is exposed as a resolvable service: the store, the target registry and the schedule executor are private to the plugin, so an application cannot bypass the authorization its Routes enforce by resolving them from the container.

### [轻微][易用性改进] Scheduler 管理 API 无“立即执行/触发”入口，验证与运维只能等待 cron

**相关对象**：`@nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler`（评审时 1.0.0-beta.16）、`app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-scheduler/references/operations.md`

**反馈证据核对**：反馈复核：证据支持

冻结源码与文档一致证实：Scheduler 插件管理 API 只有 list/get/occurrences/enable/disable，公开服务无触发方法；应用只能靠 cron '* * * * *' 等待真实 firing，浏览器验收也记录“无立即执行入口，依赖 Cron”。这是框架能力边界上的可用性改进，不涉及违约或应用缺陷。

- 职责与公开约定：冻结公开约定：scheduler Skill operations.md 列出管理入口仅 GET /schedules、GET /schedules/:scheduleId、GET /schedules/:scheduleId/occurrences、POST .../enable、POST .../disable，并明确写“The API supports viewing and enabling/disabling tasks, with no route for creating or editing Cron definitions”；验证指引要求“In development, use a short Cron interval to observe an execution”。该约定没有承诺 trigger/立即执行端点，故本候选属可用性建议而非违约。框架职责边界：插件提供管理 API 与 target/occurrence 协议，应用自建页面。；证据：E18（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-scheduler/references/operations.md:9–41）、E6（packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler/dist/server/routes/index.js:85–144）
- 实际行为证据：实现实际行为与结论相符：routes/index.js 只注册上述五条路由（enable/disable 调 scheduler.setEnabled），未注册任何 trigger/run 路由；DefaultSchedulerService 公开方法为 defineSchedule/registerTarget/list/get/listOccurrences/sync/setEnabled/activate/recordEvent/reconcileOccurrences，无 trigger 方法；应用用 cron '* * * * *' 取得真实 firing。浏览器验收 B02 直接记录“等待同一计划由真实调度再次执行（无立即执行入口，依赖 Cron * * * * *）”。因此“只能启用并等待 cron”在管理 API 范围内成立。；证据：E6（packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler/dist/server/routes/index.js:85–144）、E51（packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler/dist/server/services/scheduler.d.ts:17–38）、E52（app/server/providers/todos.ts:244–253）、E28（artifacts/verify-1/browser-acceptance/report.json:1–17）
- 应用接入与业务边界：应用未错误装配或误用 API：app/server/routes/index.ts 只挂载 todos 业务路由，未尝试解析 scheduler 私有 store/executor 或自行触发；app/server/providers/todos.ts 用 registerTarget + defineSchedule(cron '* * * * *') 正常装配，与插件公开面一致。缺入口归因于框架公开 API，而非应用遗漏业务逻辑。；证据：E53（app/server/routes/index.ts:1–8）、E52（app/server/providers/todos.ts:244–253）、E18（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-scheduler/references/operations.md:9–41）
- 环境因素：无环境因素更能解释该现象：同一环境下浏览器验收已观察到真实 cron firing（Succeeded result {"updated":1} 与 {"updated":0}），说明外部服务、jobs 适配器与运行环境都能正常触发；缺少的只是按需触发入口，而该入口在插件源码公开面本就不存在，与环境无关。；证据：E28（artifacts/verify-1/browser-acceptance/report.json:1–17）、E6（packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler/dist/server/routes/index.js:85–144）
- 工厂因素：工厂侧：scheduler 插件及其 Skill 由本轮冻结材料提供，operations.md 主动声明“no route for creating or editing Cron definitions”并建议短周期 cron 验证，未承诺 trigger；需求也把“立即执行”写成条件项（如本轮提供则须走同一 Scheduler 目标及执行记录）。因此该缺口是框架公开能力边界，不是工厂提示、注入配置或 QA/构建脚本覆盖造成的误配。；证据：E18（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-scheduler/references/operations.md:9–41）
- 已有能力与推荐用法：已核既有公开入口、正常组合与文档：schedulerServiceToken 仅暴露 registerTarget/defineSchedule 作为贡献面，管理读改经 HTTP 五路由；operations.md 明确 store、target registry、schedule executor 私有且不可由容器解析，应用无法在不绕过授权与 occurrence 的情况下触发。底层 @nocobase/jobs 的 ScheduleJobOption.immediately 仅“Fires once on creation; Ignored for updates”，且 scheduler 的 ScheduleDefinition 未暴露该字段、createScheduleDispatchJob 也未传入；CLI scheduler sync 只同步。故无既有能力满足“按需触发一次真实 firing 并写入 occurrence”。；证据：E18（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-scheduler/references/operations.md:9–41）、E51（packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler/dist/server/services/scheduler.d.ts:17–38）、E54（packages/@nocobase/jobs/dist/schedule/types.d.ts:43–53）、E55（packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler/dist/server/schedules/define.d.ts:5–20）

反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

公共接口只有 list/get/occurrences/enable/disable，没有触发一次 firing 的路由或服务方法；本轮以 cron * * * * * 等待真实触发，浏览器验收也记录了“无立即执行入口，依赖 Cron”。

**影响**：运维与验收在需要即时验证或补偿执行时只能等待下一个周期，长周期计划尤其不便。

**建议改动**：可选地在管理 API 增加受 page:scheduler.schedules/access 保护的“trigger once”端点，走同一 target 与 occurrence 记录（不作为业务写库后门）。

**验收标准**：触发后出现新的 occurrence 且 target 通过统一协议执行

**评审时证据（冻结快照）**：
- `packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler/dist/server/routes/index.js:85–144` — occurrences 游标分页、enable/disable 路由：POST 调 scheduler.setEnabled(id, true/false) 并返回更新后的 Schedule。
- `app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-scheduler/references/operations.md:9–41` — 运维指南：插件不提供页面，应用基于 GET/POST 管理路由自建页面；所有路由要求 page:scheduler.schedules/access，且先鉴权后查找；无创建/编辑 Cron 的路由。
- `artifacts/verify-1/browser-acceptance/report.json:1–17` — 浏览器验收报告 passed:true：真实 Cron 执行 result {"updated":1}，重复执行 {"updated":0} 且 todo 总数 3，停用后整页刷新仍 Disabled。

> 来源：nb3-factory 独立搭建评审（口径 v2，源 Run 38481201050 / attempt 1）。尚未复核最新上游源码。
业务实现与执行环境观察 · 不计入框架得分（1）
轻微易用性改进候选反馈：未复核原评审置信度：confirmed
F4 · Schedules 页面用浏览器本地时区渲染 nextRunAt，与标注的 Asia/Shanghai 不一致

Agent / 业务实现 · 原评审：未解决

⌄

未提供反馈复核记录，保留为候选反馈；原评审置信度不代表已排除误报。

应用页面用 Intl.DateTimeFormat(locale) 直接格式化 nextRunAt/lastRunAt；在运行环境浏览器时区与计划时区不同时，界面会显示与“timezone Asia/Shanghai”标签不符的时间（验收记录中 timezone 为 Asia/Shanghai，Next run 显示为 11:43 PM）。

实际影响
管理员可能误读下一次执行时间。
建议改法
在应用页面用计划自身的 timezone 格式化时间，或显式标注显示时区。

应有能力 / 行为
显示时间与标注的计划时区一致
实际观察

用户自行换算时区

NocoBase3 库、插件、模板与指引 2

做得好的地方
F1 · Scheduler 目标扩展点与 occurrence 幂等协议可靠支撑真实调度与去重

NocoBase3 内核 · @nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-scheduler/references/targets.md

⌄

registerTarget 是唯一目标扩展面；dispatch 以 executor jobId 作为 occurrenceId，ScheduleOccurrenceStore.start 对已存在或终态返回 noop，并发插入失败也返回 noop。应用的 app.todos.markExpired 在 boot() 注册，cron * * * * * 真实触发后 result 为 {updated:1}，重复执行为 {updated:0} 且 todo 总数不变。

提供的帮助
满足“受控短周期验证一次真实调度”和“重复执行不产生重复待办”，且幂等由框架层保证而非业务自行兜底。
应保留的能力
无需修改；可作为其他定时任务的参考实现。
E2 E3 E17 E28
做得好的地方
F2 · ServiceProvider 生命周期与容器解析顺序清晰，业务代码无需触碰插件内部

NocoBase3 内核 · @nocobase/service-provider · @nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/services-and-jobs.md

⌄

register() 绑定 token、boot() 使用其他服务；所有 register/boot 先于 scheduler.start，后者才同步 manifest 并 setup 执行器。scheduler 插件的 registry/store/executor 保持私有，仅暴露 schedulerServiceToken 与 HTTP 路由。

提供的帮助
应用组合顺序可预测，调度器与业务服务的依赖关系稳定，减少装配类缺陷。
应保留的能力
无需修改。

本次评审的覆盖限制
  • 只读评审：未启动应用、未安装依赖、未重跑迁移或调度，结论基于快照源码与归档验收记录。
  • 框架包为 dist-only（无 src），代码证据引用 dist 下 .js 文件。
  • 真实调度与幂等结论来自归档的浏览器验收报告，非本轮复现。
  • 评审者无法读取截图图像，UI 维度未评。
  • 历史错误信号均为 Agent/环境/工具层，未据此推断框架缺陷。
  • 快照未包含 1 个超出预算、非普通文件或不可读取的文件；未据此确认其实现。

E1 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler/dist/server/schedules/registry.js:1–38

目标注册表：重复 type 抛错；validate 返回 {valid:false,reason:"target-not-found"}；describe/start/inspect 按 type 解析目标。

export class ScheduleTargetRegistry {
    targets = new Map();
    register(target) {
        if (this.targets.has(target.type))
            throw new Error(`Schedule target type already registered: ${target.type}`);
        // The registry is heterogeneous: each target parses its own config out of
        // the JSON a definition declared, which `validate()` is there to check.
        this.targets.set(target.type, target);
    }
    get(type) {
        return this.targets.get(type);
    }
    validate(definition) {
        const target = this.get(definition.target.type);
        return target
            ? target.validate(definition.target.config)
            : { valid: false, reason: 'target-not-found' };
    }
    async describe(type, config) {
        const target = this.get(type);
        if (!target)
            return { targetLabel: type, state: 'missing' };
        return target.describe
            ? target.describe(config)
            : { targetLabel: target.title, state: 'ready' };
    }
    async start(type, config, context) {
        const target = this.get(type);
        if (!target)
            return { state: 'failed', reason: 'target-not-found' };
        const validation = target.validate(config);
        if (!validation.valid)
            return {
                state: 'failed',
                reason: validation.reason ?? 'invalid-config',
            };
        return target.start(config, context);
    }

原文件 SHA-256：a908b07aa1cc2de224b0a4f9cfc77422064fdcb6d25abb33cb931c31923f07b4

E2 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler/dist/server/dispatch.js:23–74

派发 Job：occurrenceId=context.jobId；目标非 ready 时 skip；occurrences.start 返回 noop 时不再执行；completed/accepted/skipped/failed 四种结果分别落库。

async function dispatch(spec, context, { targets, occurrences }) {
    // The occurrence is the firing: its id is the executor's job id, so the same
    // firing delivered twice records, and starts its target, once.
    const occurrenceId = context.jobId;
    const executionContext = { scheduleId: spec.id, occurrenceId };
    const record = {
        scheduleId: spec.id,
        definitionHash: spec.definitionHash,
        targetType: spec.target.type,
        scheduledAt: context.scheduledAt,
    };
    const targetState = (await targets.describe(spec.target.type, spec.target.config)).state;
    if (targetState && targetState !== 'ready') {
        await occurrences.skip(occurrenceId, `target-${targetState}`, record);
        return;
    }
    const action = await occurrences.start(executionContext, spec.definitionHash, spec.target.type, context.scheduledAt);
    if (action === 'noop')
        return;
    try {
        const result = await targets.start(spec.target.type, spec.target.config, executionContext);
        switch (result.state) {
            case 'completed':
                await occurrences.succeed(occurrenceId, result.result);
                return;
            case 'accepted': {
                await occurrences.wait(occurrenceId, result.reference, result.receipt);
                const observation = await targets.inspect(spec.target.type, result.reference);
                if (observation.state === 'completed')
                    await occurrences.complete(occurrenceId, result.reference, observation.completion);
                return;
            }
            case 'skipped':
                await occurrences.skip(occurrenceId, result.reason);
                return;
            case 'failed':
                await occurrences.fail(occurrenceId, result.reason);
                return;
        }
    }
    catch (error) {
        try {
            await occurrences.fail(occurrenceId, 'dispatch-failed');
        }
        catch (completionError) {
            console.error('Failed to record schedule dispatch failure', {
                occurrenceId,
                error: completionError,
            });
        }
        throw error;
    }

原文件 SHA-256：2f586836bd5eba506dcc3a01a7bbcb86c184e779712d5ea6c12bf27bb9cb2495

E3 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler/dist/server/occurrences.js:23–71

ScheduleOccurrenceStore.start：已存在且为 waiting/终态返回 noop；并发插入失败也返回 noop，保证同一 firing 只执行一次。

    async start(context, definitionHash, targetType, scheduledAt) {
        const now = new Date();
        const existing = await this.find(context.occurrenceId);
        if (existing) {
            if (existing.status === 'waiting' || TERMINAL.has(existing.status))
                return 'noop';
            const updated = await this.database
                .query()
                .updateTable('schedule_occurrences')
                .set({
                status: 'running',
                executionCount: existing.executionCount + 1,
                lastStartedAt: now,
                updatedAt: now,
            })
                .where('id', '=', context.occurrenceId)
                .where('status', 'in', ['pending', 'running'])
                .execute();
            if ((updated.updatedCount ?? 0) === 0) {
                const current = await this.required(context.occurrenceId);
                if (current.status === 'waiting' || TERMINAL.has(current.status))
                    return 'noop';
            }
            return 'start';
        }
        const inserted = await this.insertOnce({
            id: context.occurrenceId,
            scheduleId: context.scheduleId,
            definitionHash,
            status: 'running',
            reason: null,
            targetType,
            targetReferenceType: null,
            targetReferenceId: null,
            targetReceipt: null,
            resultSummary: null,
            executionCount: 1,
            scheduledAt: scheduledAt ?? null,
            startedAt: now,
            acceptedAt: null,
            lastStartedAt: now,
            lastObservedAt: null,
            finishedAt: null,
            createdAt: now,
            updatedAt: now,
        });
        // Losing the insert to a concurrent delivery of the same firing means that
        // delivery runs it.
        return inserted ? 'start' : 'noop';

E4 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler/dist/server/services/scheduler.js:14–47

DefaultSchedulerService：defineSchedule 入内存 manifest；registerTarget 返回 reportCompletion 句柄；list/get 投影 targetState 与 targetSummary。

    defineSchedule(definition) {
        this.manifest.push(Object.freeze({ definition: buildScheduleDefinition(definition) }));
    }
    registerTarget(target) {
        this.targets.register(target);
        const { type } = target;
        return {
            type,
            reportCompletion: (occurrenceId, reference, completion) => this.occurrences.complete(occurrenceId, reference, completion, type),
        };
    }
    async list() {
        const [records, targets] = await Promise.all([
            this.store.list(),
            this.store.listTargets(),
        ]);
        const byId = new Map(targets.map((target) => [target.id, target]));
        return Promise.all(records.map(async (record) => {
            const target = byId.get(record.id);
            return {
                ...record,
                targetState: target
                    ? ((await this.targets.describe(target.type, target.config))
                        .state ?? 'invalid')
                    : 'missing',
                targetSummary: target
                    ? await this.targets.describe(target.type, target.config)
                    : { targetLabel: record.targetType, state: 'missing' },
            };
        }));
    }
    /** One schedule with the same projection as `list()`, or `undefined` when this application has no such schedule. */
    async get(id) {
        return (await this.list()).find((entry) => entry.id === id);

E5 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler/dist/server/routes/index.js:34–67

管理路由鉴权：authentication.required + authorization.middleware，随后按 page:scheduler.schedules/access 校验，未授权抛 403 SCHEDULE_ACCESS_REQUIRED，且在查找 schedule 之前。

    schedules.use('*', authentication.required(), authorization.middleware());
    // Permission is checked before any schedule is looked up, so a caller without access cannot probe which ids exist.
    schedules.use('*', async (context, next) => {
        const allowed = await context.get('authz').can({
            resource: { type: 'page', id: SCHEDULER_ACCESS_RESOURCE },
            action: 'access',
        });
        if (!allowed)
            throw new ApiError({
                status: 'PERMISSION_DENIED',
                reason: 'SCHEDULE_ACCESS_REQUIRED',
                domain: SCHEDULER_ERROR_DOMAIN,
                message: 'Schedule access is required.',
            });
        await next();
    });
    schedules.get('/schedules', describeRoute({
        tags,
        summary: 'List Schedules',
        operationId: 'schedulerListSchedules',
        description: `Pages by \`page\` and \`pageSize\`, ordered by title. ${accessDescription}`,
        responses: {
            200: listResponse(ScheduleSchema, SchedulePageMeta),
            ...apiErrorResponses,
        },
    }), apiValidator('query', ScheduleListQuery), async (context) => {
        const { page, pageSize } = context.req.valid('query');
        const items = await scheduler.list();
        const start = (page - 1) * pageSize;
        return context.json({
            data: items.slice(start, start + pageSize),
            meta: { page, pageSize, total: items.length },
        });
    });

E6 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler/dist/server/routes/index.js:85–144

occurrences 游标分页、enable/disable 路由：POST 调 scheduler.setEnabled(id, true/false) 并返回更新后的 Schedule。

    schedules.get('/schedules/:scheduleId/occurrences', describeRoute({
        tags,
        summary: 'List the occurrences of a Schedule',
        operationId: 'schedulerListOccurrences',
        description: `The firings of a Schedule, newest first. Pages by \`pageToken\`: pass \`meta.nextPageToken\` back unchanged; it is absent on the last page. ${accessDescription}`,
        responses: {
            200: listResponse(ScheduleOccurrenceSchema),
            ...apiErrorResponses,
            404: scheduleNotFoundResponse,
        },
    }), scheduleParams, apiValidator('query', OccurrenceListQuery), async (context) => {
        const { scheduleId } = context.req.valid('param');
        const { pageSize, pageToken=[REDACTED] } = context.req.valid('query');
        if (!(await scheduler.get(scheduleId)))
            throw scheduleNotFound(scheduleId);
        // One row past the page tells whether another page follows without counting the whole history.
        const rows = await scheduler.listOccurrences(scheduleId, {
            offset,
            limit: pageSize + 1,
        });
        const hasMore = rows.length > pageSize;
        return context.json({
            data: rows.slice(0, pageSize),
            meta: hasMore
                ? { nextPageToken=[REDACTED] + pageSize) }
                : {},
        });
    });
    schedules.post('/schedules/:scheduleId/enable', describeRoute({
        tags,
        summary: 'Enable a Schedule',
        operationId: 'schedulerEnableSchedule',
        description: `Switches the Schedule on and plans its next firing when its lifecycle is active. ${accessDescription}`,
        responses: {
            200: dataResponse(ScheduleSchema, 'The Schedule after the change.'),
            ...apiErrorResponses,
            404: scheduleNotFoundResponse,
        },
    }), scheduleParams, async (context) => {
        const { scheduleId } = context.req.valid('param');
        return context.json({
            data: await scheduler.setEnabled(scheduleId, true),
        });
    });
    schedules.post('/schedules/:scheduleId/disable', describeRoute({
        tags,
        summary: 'Disable a Schedule',
        operationId: 'schedulerDisableSchedule',
        description: `Switches the Schedule off and removes its planned firing; the definition stays. ${accessDescription}`,
        responses: {
            200: dataResponse(ScheduleSchema, 'The Schedule after the change.'),
            ...apiErrorResponses,
            404: scheduleNotFoundResponse,
        },
    }), scheduleParams, async (context) => {
        const { scheduleId } = context.req.valid('param');
        return context.json({
            data: await scheduler.setEnabled(scheduleId, false),
        });
    });

E7 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler/dist/server/routes/schemas.js:49–111

ScheduleSchema：enabled/lifecycleState/scheduleStatus/targetState/nextRunAt/lastRunAt/runCount/completedCount 等维度分离。

const targetState = z.enum(['ready', 'disabled', 'missing', 'invalid']);
export const ScheduleSchema = z
    .object({
    id: z.string(),
    appName: z.string(),
    key: z.string().meta({
        description: 'The key the Schedule was defined with, unique within the application.',
    }),
    title: z.string(),
    description: z.string().optional(),
    cron: z
        .string()
        .meta({ description: 'The cron expression, evaluated in `timezone`.' }),
    timezone: z
        .string()
        .meta({ description: 'An IANA time zone, such as `Asia/Shanghai`.' }),
    enabled: z.boolean().meta({
        description: 'Whether an administrator has the Schedule switched on.',
    }),
    targetType: z
        .string()
        .meta({ description: 'The registered target type a firing runs.' }),
    lifecycleState: z.enum(['active', 'inactive']).meta({
        description: '`inactive` when the application no longer defines the Schedule; `inactiveReason` says why.',
    }),
    inactiveReason: z
        .string()
        .optional()
        .meta({ description: 'Such as `definition_removed`.' }),
    definitionHash: z.string(),
    runCount: z
        .number()
        .int()
        .min(0)
        .meta({ description: 'How many times the Schedule has fired.' }),
    completedCount: z
        .number()
        .int()
        .min(0)

E8 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler/dist/server/providers/scheduler.js:22–103

SchedulerProvider：register 绑定 schedulerServiceToken；start 先 sync manifest 再注册订阅并 setup 执行器；compose 从 jobExecutorServiceToken 取 scope 的 ScheduleExecutor；scheduler.jobs 命名未知配置时拒绝启动。

    register() {
        // One service reaches the container. The target registry, the executor
        // and both stores are this provider's own parts, handed to whatever needs
        // them instead of being resolvable by anyone holding the container.
        this.app.container.singleton(schedulerServiceToken, () => this.compose().service);
    }
    async start() {
        const { executor, service } = this.compose();
        const startupMode = this.app.container.resolveIfCreated(schedulerStartupModeToken);
        const consume = startupMode?.kind !== 'sync-only';
        // Registers every definition's handler, and the rules of the enabled ones,
        // before setup() writes the rules and starts the worker: a due firing must
        // never reach this instance ahead of its handler.
        await service.sync(startupMode?.finalize ?? false);
        if (consume) {
            this.unsubscribe = executor.subscribe(async (event) => {
                if (event.reason === 'handler-not-registered') {
                    // Nothing reaches the occurrence history: no dispatch ran.
                    console.warn('A schedule fired that this instance has no definition for', { scheduleId: event.jobName, occurrenceId: event.jobId });
                }
                await service.recordEvent(event);
            });
        }
        await executor.setup({ consume });
        await service.activate();
        if (!consume)
            return;
        this.reconcileTimer = setInterval(() => {
            void service.reconcileOccurrences().catch((error) => {
                console.error('Scheduler occurrence reconciliation failed', error);
            });
        }, 60_000);
        this.reconcileTimer.unref?.();
    }
    async shutdown() {
        if (this.reconcileTimer)
            clearInterval(this.reconcileTimer);
        this.reconcileTimer = undefined;
        this.unsubscribe?.();
        this.unsubscribe = undefined;
        // Waits for a running firing to finish before what it uses is released.
        // The rules stay in the backend for the other instances.
        await this.internals?.executor.shutdown();
        this.internals = undefined;
    }
    /**
     * The `jobs` configuration `scheduler.jobs` names. The jobs service would
     * fall back to `jobs.default` for a name it does not know, which would put
     * the schedules on another backend without a word, so an unknown name
     * refuses to start instead.
     */
    jobsConfiguration() {
        const name = this.app.config.get('scheduler')?.jobs;
        if (name === undefined)
            return undefined;
        const jobs = this.app.config.get('jobs');
        const selected = name === 'default' ? undefined : jobs?.[name];
        if (!selected || typeof selected !== 'object') {
            throw new Error(`scheduler.jobs names "${name}", which is not a jobs configuration.`);
        }
        return name;
    }
    compose() {
        if (this.internals)
            return this.internals;
        const container = this.app.container;
        const database = container.resolve(databaseManagerToken);
        const targets = new ScheduleTargetRegistry();
        const occurrences = new ScheduleOccurrenceStore(database);
        // Concurrency and attempts are the selected configuration's. A failed
        // dispatch is recorded as the occurrence's outcome, so a retry of the same
        // firing finds it recorded and does nothing: keep attempts at 1 there.
        const executor = container
            .resolve(jobExecutorServiceToken)
            .getScheduleExecutor(SCHEDULER_SCOPE, this.jobsConfiguration());
        const store = new ScheduleStore(database, this.app.appName, executor, (spec, limit) => createScheduleDispatchJob(spec, limit, { targets, occurrences }));
        this.internals = {
            executor,
            service: new DefaultSchedulerService(store, occurrences, targets),
        };
        return this.internals;
    }

E10 · packages/@nocobase/service-provider/dist/container.js:12–56

ServiceContainer：singleton 延迟构建、resolve 缓存、循环依赖检测与失败缓存。

    singleton(token, factory) {
        this.assertAvailable(token);
        this.bindings.set(token, {
            factory,
            instance: undefined,
            state: 'pending',
        });
    }
    has(token) {
        return this.bindings.has(token);
    }
    resolve(token) {
        const binding = this.bindings.get(token);
        if (!binding) {
            throw new Error(`Service "${token.name}" is not registered.`);
        }
        if (binding.state === 'created') {
            return binding.instance;
        }
        if (binding.state === 'failed') {
            throw binding.error;
        }
        if (binding.state === 'creating') {
            const cycle = [...this.resolving, token]
                .map((item) => item.name)
                .join(' -> ');
            throw new Error(`Circular service dependency detected: ${cycle}.`);
        }
        binding.state = 'creating';
        this.resolving.push(token);
        try {
            const value = binding.factory(this);
            binding.instance = value;
            binding.state = 'created';
            return value;
        }
        catch (error) {
            binding.error = error;
            binding.state = 'failed';
            throw error;
        }
        finally {
            this.resolving.pop();
        }
    }

Repository.updateMany：规范化过滤、校验写策略与字段，调用 adapter.updateMany 并返回 { updatedCount }（可带 records）。

    async updateMany(options) {
        const policyInput = options.writePolicy === undefined ? true : options.writePolicy;
        assertWriteEnabled(policyInput);
        const writePolicy = policyInput === true ? true : normalizeFieldWritePolicy(policyInput);
        const collection = await this.collection();
        assertWritableCollection(collection);
        // Before the payload is looked at: a Policy that forbids this operation
        // refuses the call outright, so an empty or malformed body is reported as
        // forbidden rather than as invalid input. The caller learns nothing about
        // the shape of a write they may not perform.
        resolveWriteShapeNode(this.options.policy, 'update', collection);
        if (writePolicy !== true)
            validatePolicyFields(collection, writePolicy, ['writePolicy']);
        const filter = await this.normalizeMutationFilter(collection, options.filter, options.context, options.all === true, 'update');
        const values = validateValues(collection, evaluateValues(options.values), 'updateMany', false, options.context);
        assertFieldWrites(values, writePolicy, ['values'], [], 'update', collection.name);
        this.assertPolicyMutationFields(collection, { values }, 'update', [
            'values',
        ]);
        const selection = options.select
            ? await this.validateSelect(collection, options.select, options.context, this.readContext(collection))
            : undefined;
        if (selection)
            assertBulkReturningIdentity(collection);
        const result = await this.options.adapter.updateMany({
            collection,
            meta: normalizeRepositoryEventMeta(options.meta, collection.name),
            filter,
            all: options.all === true,
            values,
            fields: selection
                ? includeExecutionFields(collection, selection.fields)
                : undefined,
            select: selection?.select,
            scopeCheck: await this.scopeCheck(collection, 'update'),
        });
        return selection
            ? {
                updatedCount: result.count,
                records: pickManySelections(result.records, selection),
            }
            : { updatedCount: result.count };

E14 · packages/@nocobase/jobs/dist/config.js:31–66

resolveJobsConfig：memory 为内置回退并带 persistencePath；redis 需要 connection；未知 adapter 抛错。

export function resolveJobsConfig(selection, scope, defaults) {
    const config = selection.config;
    const base = {
        key: selection.key,
        builtIn: config === undefined,
        scope,
        namespace: config?.namespace ?? defaults.appName,
        concurrency: positiveInteger(config?.concurrency ?? 1, 'concurrency'),
        attempts: positiveInteger(config?.attempts ?? 1, 'attempts'),
    };
    if (!base.namespace) {
        throw new Error('A jobs namespace must be a non-empty string.');
    }
    if (config === undefined || config.adapter === 'memory') {
        const configuredPath = config?.persistence?.path;
        return {
            ...base,
            adapter: 'memory',
            persistencePath: configuredPath
                ? path.resolve(configuredPath)
                : defaults.storagePath,
        };
    }
    if (config.adapter === 'redis') {
        if (!config.connection || typeof config.connection !== 'object') {
            throw new Error(`Jobs configuration "${selection.key}" needs a redis connection.`);
        }
        return {
            ...base,
            adapter: 'redis',
            connection: config.connection,
            removeOnComplete: config.removeOnComplete ?? DEFAULT_REMOVE_ON_COMPLETE,
            removeOnFail: config.removeOnFail ?? DEFAULT_REMOVE_ON_FAIL,
        };
    }
    throw new Error(`Jobs configuration "${selection.key}" uses adapter "${String(config.adapter)}", which is neither "redis" nor "memory".`);

E15 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler/dist/server/schedules/define.js:11–39

defineSchedule 校验：key 正则、title、cron 5/6 段、cron+timezone 可解析、target.type、limit、from<=to，并拒绝敏感 config 字段。

export function defineSchedule(definition) {
    if (!definition.key || !/^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._:-]*$/.test(definition.key))
        throw new Error('Schedule key must be a stable identifier.');
    if (!definition.title)
        throw new Error('Schedule title is required.');
    const parts = definition.schedule?.cron?.trim().split(/\s+/) ?? [];
    if (parts.length !== 5 && parts.length !== 6)
        throw new Error('Schedule cron must contain five or six fields.');
    const timezone = definition.schedule.timezone ?? 'UTC';
    try {
        CronExpressionParser.parse(definition.schedule.cron, {
            currentDate: new Date('2020-01-01T00:00:00.000Z'),
            tz: timezone,
        });
    }
    catch {
        throw new Error('Schedule cron or timezone is invalid.');
    }
    if (!definition.target?.type)
        throw new Error('Schedule target type is required.');
    assertNoSensitiveConfig(definition.target.config);
    if (definition.schedule.limit !== undefined &&
        (!Number.isInteger(definition.schedule.limit) ||
            definition.schedule.limit < 1))
        throw new Error('Schedule limit must be a positive integer.');
    if (definition.schedule.from &&
        definition.schedule.to &&
        definition.schedule.from > definition.schedule.to)
        throw new Error('Schedule from must not be after to.');

E16 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-scheduler/SKILL.md:10–31

Scheduler 技能：插件拥有调度投影与执行状态；registerTarget 是目标扩展面且无内置 target；需要 UI 可观测才用 Scheduler，否则用应用自己的 ScheduleExecutor。

Use the installed version's public exports, without importing plugin internals. The application owns business logic, schedule declarations, Providers, permissions, and deployment timing. Scheduler owns scheduling projections, trigger history, and execution state. The plugin's `skills/` directory is the source of truth; do not edit the synchronized application copy under `.agents/skills/`.

## Decide Whether to Use Scheduler

The primary reason to define tasks through Scheduler is **UI observability for business administrators**. Developers define schedules and execution logic in code. Business administrators use the task list and detail pages the application builds on the Scheduler API to view schedules, track individual executions, and enable or disable tasks when authorized.

Tasks that do not need to be viewed and tracked through the UI must not be managed by the Scheduler plugin. Give recurring ones a `ScheduleExecutor` of their own from the application's jobs service (`jobExecutorServiceToken` in `@nocobase/app-server/jobs`), and one-off background work a `JobExecutor`; the application development Skill describes both. For administrator-visible tasks, deliver Client registration, access permissions, meaningful task names, and accurate final execution status together; successful server-side dispatch alone is incomplete.

## Then Choose the Execution Model

After deciding to use Scheduler, choose according to business complexity:

| Scenario                                                                                  | Choice          | Implementation boundary                                                                                  |
| ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Periodic cleanup, cache refresh, a single report, or one business Service call            | Own target type | Short operations may complete directly; hand lengthy work to a `JobExecutor`                             |
| A simple operation that happens hourly                                                    | Own target type | Cron alone is not a reason to introduce a heavier execution system                                       |
| Immediate asynchronous execution                                                          | `JobExecutor`   | No Cron Schedule is needed                                                                               |
| A one-time delay, such as ten minutes after a request                                     | Queue `delay`   | No Cron Schedule is needed; `JobExecutor` has no delay                                                   |
| Another execution system with its own references, status queries, and completion protocol | Own target type | The same `registerTarget()` call, with `inspect()` and completion reporting for work that finishes later |

Every schedule points at a registered target; `registerTarget()` is the target extension surface, and there is no built-in target type. A target does not guarantee exactly-once external business effects; design business idempotency.

原文件 SHA-256：b068028d4f469d254eff36dd9aa38d62674285d25825a2f9c61937b762bb076d

E17 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-scheduler/references/targets.md:1–52

目标协议指南：在 Provider.boot() 中注册；validate 校验 config；重复 type 抛错；短任务可直接 completed，长任务交给 JobExecutor 返回 accepted；用 occurrenceId 去重。

# Target Extensions and Execution Protocol

A schedule points at a target, and a target is the plugin's one extension point: `scheduler.registerTarget(target)` declares how a configuration is validated, how a firing starts, and — for work that finishes later — how a run is inspected and reported. Register targets in their owning Provider's `boot()`: all Providers have registered services, and Scheduler has not yet synchronized the manifest in `start()`. Do not resolve the container during module import or depend on another Provider's boot order to create services. Duplicate target types throw.

## Add an Ordinary Scheduled Task

This short task example can be used independently. Create `server/providers/scheduled-log.ts` in the application and add the Provider to its existing Provider array:

```ts
import { loggingToken } from '@nocobase/app-server/logging';
import type { AppPluginApplication } from '@nocobase/app-server/plugins';
import { schedulerServiceToken } from '@nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler/server/tokens';
import { ServiceProvider } from '@nocobase/service-provider';

export default class ScheduledLogProvider extends ServiceProvider<AppPluginApplication> {
  public readonly name = 'app/scheduled-log';

  public override async boot(): Promise<void> {
    const scheduler = this.app.container.resolve(schedulerServiceToken);
    const logger = this.app.container.resolve(loggingToken).getLogger();
    scheduler.registerTarget({
      type: 'app.scheduled-log',
      title: 'Scheduled time report',
      validate(config) {
        return config !== null &&
          typeof config === 'object' &&
          !Array.isArray(config) &&
          typeof (config as { message?: unknown }).message === 'string'
          ? { valid: true }
          : { valid: false, reason: 'message-must-be-a-string' };
      },
      async start(config, context) {
        logger.info(
          {
            message: config.message,
            scheduleId: context.scheduleId,
            occurrenceId: context.occurrenceId,
          },
          'Scheduled log',
        );
        return { state: 'completed', outcome: 'succeeded' };
      },
    });
  }
}
```

Use target `{ type: 'app.scheduled-log', config: { message: 'Time report' } }`. For business work, replace logging with a domain Service call and validate the complete config. Resolve Scheduler directly when it is required; use `container.has()` to skip registration only for a truly optional integration.

Namespace the type so it cannot collide with another plugin's: `app.` for an application's own tasks, the plugin name for a plugin's. Two registrations of the same type throw at boot.

Short operations may complete inside `start()`, but occupy the schedule worker. Hand lengthy work to a `JobExecutor` and return `accepted`, as the next section shows. Do not let config select arbitrary module paths, job names, queue names or channels.

原文件 SHA-256：1ac583f42b60c67926ad86915a6ddb5f8ccd561b98a59e445944cfdade3f9186

E18 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-scheduler/references/operations.md:9–41

运维指南：插件不提供页面，应用基于 GET/POST 管理路由自建页面；所有路由要求 page:scheduler.schedules/access，且先鉴权后查找；无创建/编辑 Cron 的路由。

## Management Entry Points

The plugin contributes no pages: the application builds its schedule list and detail pages on the routes below and declares them among its own routes, guarded by the same permission. Definitions are maintained in source. The API supports viewing and enabling/disabling tasks, with no route for creating or editing Cron definitions.

All routes below require authentication and authorization for `{ resource: { type: 'page', id: 'scheduler.schedules' }, action: 'access' }`, checked before the schedule is looked up. Enabling/disabling currently uses the same permission, not a separate update action.

| Method and path                                        | Response / effect                                                                                                                                                  |
| ------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `GET /api/scheduler/schedules`                         | `{ data: ScheduleListItem[], meta: { page, pageSize, total } }`; `page` (default 1) and `pageSize` (default 20, at most 100)                                       |
| `GET /api/scheduler/schedules/:scheduleId`             | `{ data: ScheduleListItem }`                                                                                                                                       |
| `GET /api/scheduler/schedules/:scheduleId/occurrences` | `{ data: ScheduleOccurrenceView[], meta: { nextPageToken? } }`, newest first; `pageSize` (default 20, at most 100) and `pageToken` from the previous page's `meta` |
| `POST /api/scheduler/schedules/:scheduleId/enable`     | `{ data: ScheduleListItem }`; enables the task, no request body                                                                                                    |
| `POST /api/scheduler/schedules/:scheduleId/disable`    | `{ data: ScheduleListItem }`; pauses the task, no request body                                                                                                     |

Failures use the standard `/api` error body with domain `scheduler`: a caller without page access gets `403` with reason `SCHEDULE_ACCESS_REQUIRED`, an id this application has no schedule for gets `404` with reason `SCHEDULE_NOT_FOUND`, and an invalid `page`, `pageSize` or `pageToken` gets `400` with reason `INVALID_INPUT`. Treat `pageToken` as opaque; the last page has no `nextPageToken`.

The running application documents these routes under the `Scheduler` tag at `/api/swagger/docs` (JSON at `/api/swagger`, signed in), with the full `ScheduleListItem` and occurrence schemas; their operationIds are `schedulerListSchedules`, `schedulerGetSchedule`, `schedulerListOccurrences`, `schedulerEnableSchedule` and `schedulerDisableSchedule`.

Use the id returned by list for `:scheduleId`, not the definition key or a target config value. Enabling/disabling affects future scheduling; it does not cancel work already started.

`schedulerServiceToken` from `@nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler/server/tokens` is the plugin's one public service, and it is a contribution surface rather than an administration one:

```ts
registerTarget<TConfig extends JsonObject>(
  target: ScheduleTargetType<TConfig>,
): ScheduleTargetHandle;

defineSchedule(definition: ScheduleDefinition): void;
```

The returned handle carries `reportCompletion(occurrenceId, reference, completion)`, and completes only occurrences its own target started. `defineSchedule` has no return value; it validates and stores the definition in the in-memory manifest consumed by the next sync.

Reading and changing schedules — list, occurrences, enable and disable — is reachable through the HTTP API above, and synchronization through `pnpm nocobase scheduler sync`. Neither is exposed as a resolvable service: the store, the target registry and the schedule executor are private to the plugin, so an application cannot bypass the authorization its Routes enforce by resolving them from the container.

原文件 SHA-256：cc4daee55a38ba0b618b6105a3ca9d0ce9a9ceb173c73266685093e823b00999

E19 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-scheduler/references/definitions.md:56–69

定义字段契约：key/title/cron/timezone/from/to/limit/target 的规则；defineSchedule 校验并冻结；同步成功不代表业务行为正确。

| Field                   | Current contract                                                                                                                                                           |
| ----------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `key`                   | Required; matches `^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._:-]*$`; unique within the application. Application name and key form persistent identity, so renaming is not an in-place update |
| `title` / `description` | Required title / optional description                                                                                                                                      |
| `schedule.cron`         | Five fields starting with minutes, or six starting with seconds; `*/5 * * * *` runs every five minutes, `*/10 * * * * *` every ten seconds                                 |
| `schedule.timezone`     | Defaults to `UTC`; use an explicit IANA timezone and consider daylight saving changes for local business time                                                              |
| `schedule.from` / `to`  | Optional inclusive `Date` boundaries; construct from ISO timestamps with explicit timezone offsets; from must not exceed to                                                |
| `schedule.limit`        | Optional positive integer; counts firings started, not successful completions. Disabling and re-enabling or changing the definition continues from the firings already run |
| `target.type`           | A target type the application or a plugin registered. There is no built-in type                                                                                            |
| `target.config`         | JSON object; no functions, Service instances, or credentials. Sensitive field names are rejected recursively                                                               |

`defineSchedule()` validates and normalizes the definition before adding it to the in-memory manifest for the next sync. It defaults the timezone, computes a definition hash, and freezes the stored definition. Do not construct the hash manually or mutate a definition after passing it in. There are no `enabled`, `retry`, or `overlap` declaration fields; do not insert job or queue options, or fields from another scheduling framework.

`target.config` is whatever the target's own `validate()` accepts. Synchronization checks that the type is registered and runs that `validate()`; anything it does not check is checked when the schedule fires. Successful synchronization does not prove the config drives correct business behavior.

E20 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-db/SKILL.md:88–113

数据层指南：迁移用 defineMigration({name, up({builder}), down}) 与 builder.createCollection；示例展示字段、索引声明。

A migration creates structure. A seed inserts the records the application needs in order to run. Neither does the other's job. Sample or demonstration content is not install data: declare it as a sample seed, described below, never as a plain seed.

```ts

E21 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/services-and-jobs.md:29–60

应用组合指南：ServiceProvider 示例、token 身份规则、singleton/resolve，以及 register() 绑定、boot() 使用其他服务的生命周期表。

export default class OrderProvider extends ServiceProvider<Application> {
  public readonly name: string = 'app/order-provider';

  public override register(): void {
    this.app.container.singleton(orderServiceToken, () => {
      const database = this.app.container.resolve(databaseManagerToken);
      return createOrderService(database);
    });
  }
}
```

Add the provider to the array in `server/providers/index.ts` and export its token from there so routes can import it.

Resolve it where you need it:

```ts
const orders = app.container.resolve(orderServiceToken);
```

**The token is the identity.** Two `createServiceToken` calls with the same name are two different keys — the container matches by object identity. Always import the token from where it is defined rather than recreating one.

`singleton` builds the service once, on first resolve. Use `instance` for an already-constructed value.

Keep the layers apart: a service should not read a Hono context, return HTTP status codes, or decide retry behavior. It takes its dependencies and returns domain results.

## Provider lifecycle

| Method       | Runs                             | For                                              |
| ------------ | -------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------ |
| `register()` | Assembly, before anything starts | Binding tokens. Do not connect or start anything |
| `boot()`     | After all providers registered   | Work needing other services                      |

原文件 SHA-256：873a1e7927f6468fefc75876dfa24b63b138a54446bc2636d5bef95a298a15d4

E22 · app/server/providers/todos.ts:176–187

应用实现 markExpired：repository.updateMany 过滤 completed.isFalse + expired.isFalse + dueAt.before(now)，写 expired:true，返回 { updated: result.updatedCount }。

    async markExpired(now = new Date()) {
      const result = await repository.updateMany({
        filter: (filter) =>
          filter.and([
            filter.boolean('completed').isFalse(),
            filter.boolean('expired').isFalse(),
            filter.date('dueAt').before(now),
          ]),
        values: { expired: true, updatedAt: new Date() },
      });
      return { updated: result.updatedCount };
    },

E23 · app/server/providers/todos.ts:191–254

TodosProvider：register 绑定 todosServiceToken；boot 先 container.has(schedulerServiceToken) 守卫，再 registerTarget(app.todos.markExpired) 与 defineSchedule(todos.checkExpired, cron * * * * *, timezone Asia/Shanghai)。

export default class TodosProvider extends ServiceProvider<Application> {
  public readonly name = 'app/todos-provider';

  public override register(): void {
    this.app.container.singleton(todosServiceToken, () => {
      const database = this.app.container.resolve(databaseManagerToken);
      const ids = this.app.container.resolve(idGeneratorToken);
      return createTodoService({
        repository: database.repository<TodoRecord, TodoCreate, TodoUpdate>(
          'todos',
        ),
        generateId: () => ids.generateString(),
      });
    });
  }

  /**
   * Registers the target the schedule points at and declares the schedule itself.
   *
   * Both happen in `boot()`: every provider's `register()` runs before any provider's `boot()`, so the scheduler
   * service token is bound by the time this runs, and the scheduler's own `start()` (which reconciles registered
   * definitions into the database) runs after every `boot()`.
   */
  public override async boot(): Promise<void> {
    // A runtime composed without the Scheduler plugin still boots the application; there is simply no schedule to
    // declare. The application's own runtime always registers the plugin, so this only covers isolated runtimes.
    if (!this.app.container.has(schedulerServiceToken)) {
      return;
    }
    const scheduler = this.app.container.resolve(schedulerServiceToken);

    scheduler.registerTarget({
      type: TODOS_MARK_EXPIRED_TARGET,
      title: 'Check expired todos',
      validate: () => ({ valid: true }),
      describe: async () => ({
        targetLabel: 'Check expired todos',
        description:
          'Marks every open todo whose due time has passed as expired. Completed and future-dated todos are left alone.',
        href: '/todos',
        state: 'ready',
      }),
      start: async (): Promise<ScheduleTargetStartResult> => {
        const todos = this.app.container.resolve(todosServiceToken);
        const { updated } = await todos.markExpired();
        return {
          state: 'completed',
          outcome: 'succeeded',
          result: { updated },
        };
      },
    });

    scheduler.defineSchedule({
      key: TODOS_CHECK_EXPIRED_SCHEDULE,
      title: 'Check expired todos',
      description:
        'Marks every open todo whose due time has passed as expired. Completed and future-dated todos are left alone.',
      // A short cycle on purpose: the application is verified by watching one real run happen. The timezone is the
      // business timezone, so the minute boundaries line up with how the due times are read.
      schedule: { cron: '* * * * *', timezone: 'Asia/Shanghai' },
      target: { type: TODOS_MARK_EXPIRED_TARGET, config: {} },
    });
  }

E24 · app/client/pages/schedules/index.tsx:109–198

应用自建 Scheduler 页面：调 scheduler/schedules 与 occurrences；POST enable/disable；用 Intl.DateTimeFormat(locale) 渲染 nextRunAt/lastRunAt。

/**
 * Route `/schedules`: the application's own Scheduler management page.
 *
 * The scheduler plugin contributes no page, only the API and the authorization catalog, so this page is built on
 * `/api/scheduler/schedules`. It shows every registered schedule with the three things the feature has to preserve —
 * its name, whether it is enabled, and the record of what each firing did — plus the enable/disable switch the API
 * offers. The "check expired todos" schedule appears here with its history; the plugin owns every write, so nothing
 * on this page writes a todo directly.
 */
export default function SchedulesPage(): ReactElement {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const { locale } = useLocale();
  const api = useApiClient();
  const [reloadCount, setReloadCount] = useState(0);
  const [busyId, setBusyId] = useState<string>();
  const [result, setResult] = useState<{
    readonly key: string;
    readonly schedules?: readonly Schedule[];
    readonly occurrences?: Readonly<Record<string, readonly Occurrence[]>>;
    readonly error?: unknown;
  }>();

  const requestKey = String(reloadCount);
  useEffect(() => {
    const controller = new AbortController();
    const key = String(reloadCount);
    api
      .request<ScheduleList>({
        path: 'scheduler/schedules',
        query: { pageSize: 100 },
        signal: controller.signal,
      })
      .then(async ({ data }) => {
        const histories = await Promise.all(
          data.map(async (schedule) => {
            const page = await api.request<OccurrenceList>({
              path: `scheduler/schedules/${schedule.id}/occurrences`,
              query: { pageSize: 5 },
              signal: controller.signal,
            });
            return [schedule.id, page.data] as const;
          }),
        );
        if (!controller.signal.aborted) {
          setResult({
            key,
            schedules: data,
            occurrences: Object.fromEntries(histories),
          });
        }
      })
      .catch((error: unknown) => {
        if (!controller.signal.aborted) {
          setResult((previous) => ({ ...previous, key, error }));
        }
      });
    return () => controller.abort();
  }, [api, reloadCount]);

  const loading = result?.key !== requestKey;
  const error = loading ? undefined : result?.error;
  const schedules = result?.schedules;
  const occurrences = result?.occurrences ?? {};

  const formatDate = useMemo(
    () =>
      new Intl.DateTimeFormat(locale, {
        dateStyle: 'medium',
        timeStyle: 'short',
      }),
    [locale],
  );
  const formatDateTime = (value: string | undefined): string =>
    value ? formatDate.format(new Date(value)) : t('schedules.never');

  const setEnabled = useCallback(
    async (schedule: Schedule, enabled: boolean): Promise<void> => {
      setBusyId(schedule.id);
      try {
        await api.request({
          path: `scheduler/schedules/${schedule.id}/${enabled ? 'enable' : 'disable'}`,
          method: 'POST',
        });
      } finally {
        setBusyId(undefined);
        setReloadCount((count) => count + 1);
      }
    },
    [api],
  );

E27 · app/database/main/seeds/202610130002_seed_todos.ts:38–89

种子：3 条样例（过期未完成/未来/已完成），用 upsertOne(filter.reference) 幂等写入，重跑不新增。

const seed: SeedDefinition = defineSeed({
  name: '202610130002_seed_todos',

  async run(context) {
    const todos = context.repository<
      TodoRecord,
      TodoRecord,
      Partial<TodoRecord>
    >('todos');
    const now = new Date();
    const samples: readonly SampleTodo[] = [
      {
        id: 'sample-todo-past-due',
        reference: 'sample-todo-past-due',
        title: 'Submit the quarterly report',
        dueAt: new Date(now.getTime() - 2 * DAY),
        completed: false,
      },
      {
        id: 'sample-todo-future',
        reference: 'sample-todo-future',
        title: 'Prepare the team offsite agenda',
        dueAt: new Date(now.getTime() + 7 * DAY),
        completed: false,
      },
      {
        id: 'sample-todo-completed',
        reference: 'sample-todo-completed',
        title: 'Renew the domain certificate',
        dueAt: new Date(now.getTime() - DAY),
        completed: true,
      },
    ];

    for (const sample of samples) {
      await todos.upsertOne({
        filter: { reference: sample.reference },
        create: {
          id: sample.id,
          title: sample.title,
          dueAt: sample.dueAt,
          completed: sample.completed,
          expired: false,
          reference: sample.reference,
          createdAt: now,
          updatedAt: now,
        },
        update: { reference: sample.reference },
      });
    }
  },
});

This Skill decides which API to reach for, where the code belongs, and the boundaries the types cannot express.\n\n## Where the code lives\n\n```text\nserver/config/database.ts          connections and the default connection\nconfig.yml                         per-environment overrides and credentials (gitignored)\ndatabase/<connection>/migrations   schema history for that connection\ndatabase/<connection>/seeds        install data for that connection\ndatabase/<connection>/collections  resolved Collection artifacts\nserver/routes/, server/providers   runtime reads and writes\n```\n\n`<connection>` is a connection name from `server/config/database.ts`, so the default connection's migrations are in `database/main/migrations/`. Adding a connection means adding its directory alongside and keeping `database/tsconfig.json` in place, so migrations and seeds are typechecked and linted as server source.\n\nRuntime code never changes schema, and a migration never imports runtime code. That split is what these directories encode.\n\n## 1. E51 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler/dist/server/services/scheduler.d.ts:17–38

DefaultSchedulerService 公开方法为 defineSchedule/registerTarget/list/get/listOccurrences/sync/setEnabled/activate/recordEvent/reconcileOccurrences，无 trigger/立即执行方法。

export declare class DefaultSchedulerService implements SchedulerService {
    private readonly store;
    private readonly occurrences;
    private readonly targets;
    private readonly manifest;
    constructor(store: ScheduleStore, occurrences: ScheduleOccurrenceStore, targets: ScheduleTargetRegistry);
    defineSchedule(definition: ScheduleDefinition): void;
    registerTarget<TConfig extends JsonObject>(target: ScheduleTargetType<TConfig>): ScheduleTargetHandle;
    list(): Promise<readonly ScheduleListItem[]>;
    /** One schedule with the same projection as `list()`, or `undefined` when this application has no such schedule. */
    get(id: string): Promise<ScheduleListItem | undefined>;
    listOccurrences(scheduleId: string, range?: ScheduleOccurrenceRange): Promise<readonly ScheduleOccurrenceView[]>;
    sync(finalize?: boolean): Promise<void>;
    setEnabled(id: string, enabled: boolean): Promise<ScheduleListItem>;
    /** Finishes a sync that ran before the executor was set up; see `ScheduleStore.activate`. */
    activate(): Promise<void>;
    /** Records a firing this instance ran on its schedule's run state. */
    recordEvent(event: ScheduleEvent): Promise<void>;
    /** Observes occurrences whose target completed without notifying us. */
    reconcileOccurrences(): Promise<number>;
}

E52 · app/server/providers/todos.ts:244–253

应用以 registerTarget + defineSchedule 装配，计划用 cron '* * * * *' 取得真实 firing，未提供立即执行/触发入口。

    scheduler.defineSchedule({
      key: TODOS_CHECK_EXPIRED_SCHEDULE,
      title: 'Check expired todos',
      description:
        'Marks every open todo whose due time has passed as expired. Completed and future-dated todos are left alone.',
      // A short cycle on purpose: the application is verified by watching one real run happen. The timezone is the
      // business timezone, so the minute boundaries line up with how the due times are read.
      schedule: { cron: '* * * * *', timezone: 'Asia/Shanghai' },
      target: { type: TODOS_MARK_EXPIRED_TARGET, config: {} },
    });

export interface ScheduleJobOption {

    readonly limit?: number;

    readonly endDate?: Date;
    /** IANA time zone for `cron`; UTC when omitted. */

E55 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler/dist/server/schedules/define.d.ts:5–20

export interface ScheduleDefinition {
    readonly key: string;
    readonly title: string;
    readonly description?: string;
    readonly schedule: {
        readonly cron: string;
        readonly timezone?: string;
        readonly from?: Date;
        readonly to?: Date;
        readonly limit?: number;
    };
    readonly target: {
        readonly type: string;
        readonly config: JsonObject;
    };
}

What was delivered

业务场景与交付说明

过期待办标记

真实 Cron 调度执行结果 {"updated":1}，仅“Submit the quarterly report”（过期且未完成）变为 Expired，已完成与未来待办保持 Not expired。

重复执行幂等

同一目标后续多次执行结果均为 {"updated":0}，待办总数保持 3 条，状态不变。

配置持久化

在既有 Schedules 页停用计划后立即生效，整页刷新后仍显示 Disabled、Next run Never。

01管理员登录 NocoBase
→
02Todos 页确认 3 条样例（过期/已完成/未来）
→
03Schedules 页确认计划启用与 Cron
→
04等待真实调度并刷新查看执行记录 {"updated":1}
→
05Todos 页确认仅过期未完成被标记
→
06再执行同目标 {"updated":0} 且总数不变
→
07停用计划并整页刷新确认持久化

业务顺序示意，操作证据见逐项验收。

原始验收要求

启用计划并观察一次真实调度，执行记录显示结果；只有过期且未完成的记录被标记。

实际操作

  1. 以 nocobase 管理员登录 NocoBase
  2. 打开 Todos 页面确认 3 条记录：过期未完成 1、已完成 1、未来 1
  3. 打开 Schedules 页面确认“Check expired todos”计划已启用，Cron * * * * *，目标 Task=Check expired todos
  4. 等待真实调度执行并点击 Refresh
  5. 查看 Recent executions 执行记录

观察结果

  1. 计划启用状态 Enabled，Next run 显示，Last run Oct 13 2026 11:44 PM，Runs 2
  2. 执行记录：Succeeded 11:43 PM result {"updated":1}，Succeeded 11:44 PM result {"updated":0}
  3. Todos 页面：Submit the quarterly report (Due Oct 11, Open) 变为 Expired；Renew the domain certificate (Completed) 仍 Not expired；Prepare the team offsite agenda (未来) 仍 Not expired

来源：verify-1/browser-acceptance/report.json · 原始状态：passed

02

再执行同一目标一次，待办总数不增加，状态仍正确。

状态保持：Submit the quarterly report=Expired，Renew the domain certificate=Not expired(Completed)，Prepare the team offsite agenda=Not expired

A2 · 4 条操作 · 3 条观察 · 2 张证据
Agent 报告通过
⌄

原始验收要求

再执行同一目标一次，待办总数不增加，状态仍正确。

实际操作

  1. 在 Schedules 页面记录基线：Runs 2，Todos 共 3 条
  2. 等待同一“Check expired todos”计划由真实调度再次执行（无立即执行入口，依赖 Cron * * * * *）
  3. 点击 Refresh 查看新增执行记录
  4. 回到 Todos 页面统计记录数并核对状态

观察结果

  1. 同一目标又执行两次：11:45 PM result {"updated":0}、11:46 PM result {"updated":0}，Runs 由 2 增至 4
  2. Todos 页面 tbody 行数仍为 3（总数未增加）
  3. 状态保持：Submit the quarterly report=Expired，Renew the domain certificate=Not expired(Completed)，Prepare the team offsite agenda=Not expired

来源：verify-1/browser-acceptance/report.json · 原始状态：passed

A3 · 3 条操作 · 2 条观察 · 2 张证据
Agent 报告通过
⌄

原始验收要求

在既有设置页停用计划并刷新，启用状态正确保存；本项只评价配置持久化，不冒称已验证长期停用。 无法实际完成时如实记录 failed、blocked 或 not_run，不以源码检查代替浏览器通过。

实际操作

  1. 在既有 Schedules 设置页点击计划“Check expired todos”的 Disable 按钮
  2. 观察页面显示 Disabled / Enable 按钮 / Next run Never
  3. 对 /main/schedules 整页刷新并重新读取启用状态

观察结果

  1. 停用后立即显示 Check expired todos Disabled，按钮由 Disable 变为 Enable，Next run 由时间变为 Never
  2. 刷新后仍显示 Disabled、Enable 按钮与 Next run Never，Last run/Runs 历史保留（仅评价本次配置持久化，未观察长期停用）

来源：verify-1/browser-acceptance/report.json · 原始状态：passed
